URL: https://www.upwork.com/jobs/Make-com-and-n8n-expert-for-automating-article-publishing_~022022881741091218100/?referrer_url_path=find_work_home
Description: Overview I’m a one-person business. I am an author, consultant, and researcher building a repeatable AI “editorial assistant” that behaves like a graduate‑student intern: it is given a topic, ingests my existing work, performs additional research, structures an outline, and delivers high‑quality drafts for me to write from and refine. I need an automation expert in Make.com or n8n to design and implement the orchestration and integration layers. ----- Scope of work. The system should: - Ingest and organize topics - - Each topic record includes: title, issue, audience, format (Substack article, chapter, talk), target length, Outline Doc URL, Claude Draft Doc URL, Don Voice ChatGPT Doc URL, Perplexity Check URL - Pull from my existing corpus - Connect to where my content lives (Substack, Google Drive, PDFs, transcripts, etc.). - Retrieve relevant content based on the topic. - Pass this content into LLM calls as “source material” with clear labels. - Add external research by calling one or more research/LLM APIs to gather current, cited sources on the topic. - Return structured summaries plus citation metadata (URLs, titles, dates). - Generate outline and draft -----Orchestration: - Watch a “Topics” database (Google Sheets) for new topics. - Propose a detailed outline on the new topic (NotebookLM) - Do extended research on relevant sources (Perplexity) - Create a preliminary draft document (Claude) and put in Google Docs with: title, outline, full draft, and a source appendix. (Claude?) - Generate an article in my established voice and style, using my corpus as the primary foundation (ChatGPT) - Store the article (Google docs) - Update the topic record in Google sheets with draft status and document link. - Notify me via email when a draft is ready. ----- Deliverables: Advise and help me design and implement this system - Working automation scenario(s) in Make.com or n8n that run end‑to‑end from new topic → draft ready. - Configuration of connections to my existing tools. - How to add/edit prompts, change models, or tweak the workflow. - How to run, pause, and debug the system. ----- Required skills - Proven experience designing complex automations in Make.com and/or n8n (please share specific examples). - Comfort integrating with LLM and/or research APIs (OpenAI/Anthropic/Perplexity/Gemini, etc.). - Experience with content or editorial workflows is a strong plus. - Ability to work with my existing tools: Google Workspace, Perpexity, ChatGPT, Claude, Grok Nice to have: - Substack experience - Familiarity with retrieval‑augmented generation (RAG) or vector databases for using my own corpus as primary source material. - Experience encoding and enforcing a specific writing voice/style via prompts and examples. - Light dev skills (TypeScript/Python) for custom webhooks or micro‑services, if needed. ----- What I will provide - Access to example articles, book chapters, decks, and transcripts to define “my voice.” - Access to my topic backlog and content repositories. - Initial prompt ideas and “editorial logic” for how I want the system to behave. ----- Engagement details - Please include in your proposal: - 2–3 relevant past projects (links or short descriptions). - Which platform you recommend (Make.com vs n8n) and why for my use case. - Rough estimate for time to traing me and help deliver a v1 that can go from topic to draft. - Your hourly rate You’ll be working directly with me (the author/PI), so I’m looking for someone who can move quickly, communicate clearly, and isn’t afraid to suggest better ways to structure the system. more about "Make.com and n8n expert for automating article publishing"

Description: Upright Education is hiring an Instructor for our AI Agents & Workflow Automation Certificate Course. This is a short-term, part-time contract role designed for experienced AI practitioners who actively use AI and automation tools in professional, real-world environments. This role is best suited for professionals who apply AI to optimize workflows, build automations, improve business processes, or deliver client outcomes, not for casual or purely experimental AI use. About the Role You’ll teach adult learners who are new to AI but eager to apply automation skills in their careers. The course emphasizes practical implementation, real-world use cases, and applied systems thinking, rather than theory alone. You’ll lead one live session per week and support learners asynchronously as they build toward a capstone project focused on applied AI automation. The course uses Zapier as the primary workflow automation tool, with additional exposure to related platforms and concepts. This initial cohort runs for 8 weeks, with the potential to teach additional cohorts starting on different evenings. Zapier is the main tool used throughout the course - Ability to explain complex systems and workflows clearly to non-technical audiences - Strong facilitation and communication skills - Comfort providing thoughtful, constructive feedback to adult learners - Reliable availability on Wednesday evenings during the cohort Nice to Have - Prior teaching, mentoring, or professional training experience - Experience deploying AI automation in client-facing, production, or revenue-generating contexts - Background in marketing operations, RevOps, consulting, entrepreneurship, or product operations more about "Instructor – AI Agents & Workflow Automation (8-Week Online Course)"

Potential integration targets: • MS Office 365 Suite • Slack • CRM (Salesforce) • Email workflows • Internal documentation systems (SharePoint) You will: • design structured inputs/outputs so workflows are repeatable • ensure solutions are easy to use inside existing team habits • minimize friction and maximize adoption 4) End User Enablement, Training & Support Building tools is not enough—adoption is a requirement. You will: • Train end users across teams on: o how to use each GPT/workflow o prompting best practices o how to troubleshoot common issues o when NOT to use AI o safe data handling expectations • Create enablement materials: o quick start guides o example prompts and templates o short internal SOPs and cheat sheets • Provide ongoing support: o office hours o feedback intake o continuous iteration 5) Governance, Safety, and Standardization Essence operates in a sensitive, compliance-aware environment. The consultant must be able to build responsibly. URL: https://www.upwork.com/jobs/Build-Aviation-Advisor-Chatbot-MVP_~022023119307515957595/?referrer_url_path=find_work_home
Description: Project Overview: We are building a new AI-powered platform in the private aviation space. Our goal is to help high-net-worth individuals make smarter, data-driven decisions about flying private. We are starting with a simple, powerful MVP: an expert AI chatbot that can answer any question about the private aviation industry. This chatbot will be the "brain" of our future platform. The MVP: Expert Advisor Chatbot We need you to build a web-based chatbot that can act as an expert on private aviation. The user should be able to ask it complex questions, and the bot should provide accurate, nuanced answers based on a set of documents we provide. Core Task: Build a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) system. How it will work: 1. The Knowledge Base: We will provide you with a collection of ~50 documents (PDFs, .txt files) containing everything from jet card contracts and pricing sheets to articles on safety ratings and market analysis. 2. The Brain (Your Part): You will use a vector database (like Pinecone, ChromaDB, or similar) to ingest and index this knowledge base. 3. The Interface (Your Part): You will build a simple, clean web interface with a single chat window. 4. The Conversation: When a user asks a question (e.g., "What are the pros and cons of a Sentient Jet card vs. a NetJets card for flights under 2 hours?"), your system will: a. Query the vector database to find the most relevant document chunks. b. Feed that context into a large language model (like GPT-4) via an API call. c. Stream the AI-generated answer back to the user in the chat interface. Technical Requirements: • Backend: Python (FastAPI or Flask is fine). • AI: Experience with the OpenAI API (or similar) is a must. • Vector Database: Proven experience with at least one vector DB (Pinecone, Chroma, etc.) and embedding models. • Frontend: Simple HTML/CSS/JavaScript. No complex design is needed, just a clean, functional chat interface. A framework like React or Vue is acceptable if it's faster for you, but not required. • Deployment: You must be able to deploy this as a simple, password-protected web app on a service like Heroku or Render. What You Will Deliver: 1. A fully functional, deployed web application. 2. A simple admin interface where we can upload/manage the source documents for the knowledge base. 3. All source code, deployed and documented. Who We Are Looking For: • An independent, expert developer who has built RAG systems before. • Someone who understands the prompt engineering required to get high-quality answers. • You are comfortable working fast and can deliver a polished MVP within the 2-week timeline. To Apply: Please start your proposal with the word "AVIATION" so I know you've read the entire post. In your proposal, please briefly describe a similar RAG or AI chatbot project you have completed and what technologies you used. more about "Build an AI Aviation Advisor Chatbot (MVP)"

URL: https://www.upwork.com/jobs/High-Level-Workflow-Architect-Specialist_~022021997020974443459/?referrer_url_path=find_work_home
Description: GoHighLevel Customer Journey, Lead Qualification, Automation & SOP Documentation PROJECT SUMMARY (PLEASE READ BEFORE APPLYING) We are a service-based landscaping and design/build company seeking a systems-minded consultant to analyze, implement, and fully document our lead and early customer journey, using GoHighLevel as our primary customer hub. Our current stack includes: - GoHighLevel (GHL) — CRM, pipelines, communication, automation (Customer relation primary hub) - Jobber — Quotes, jobs, scheduling, invoicing (Looking to transition invoicing and quoting to GHL) - Zapier — Integration layer where required - Notion — SOP and operations documentation (main operational hub) We currently have automations in place, but they have become inconsistent and difficult to maintain. The first priority of this project is to evaluate what exists, simplify where necessary, and rebuild workflows intentionally. This is not an automation-only project. The objective is to create a clear, documented, and maintainable system that non-technical staff can operate, troubleshoot, and maintain independently after implementation. The end goal is to eliminate long-term dependency on outside consultants for routine system maintenance. PROJECT OBJECTIVE Design and implement a system where: - All leads enter GoHighLevel as the primary customer hub - Prospects move through a clear pre-qualification and expectation-setting journey - Automated communication improves clarity and filters poor-fit inquiries early - Different customer types follow separate customer journeys (new customer, existing customer, design/build (new customer or existing customer) - Pipelines reflect customer decision stages, not internal activities - Staff understand what happens next at every stage - Automations support operations without removing human control - The system remains understandable and recoverable after implementation The goal is clarity, qualification, durability, and simplicity, not maximum automation. You will be expected to simplify or remove unnecessary automation where appropriate. SCOPE OF WORK 🔹 Phase 0 — System Audit & Customer Journey Architecture (Required First) Before implementation begins, the consultant will perform a full audit and architecture phase. This phase must be completed and approved before implementation begins. 1️⃣ Existing Automation Audit Review current GHL, Jobber, and Zapier automations and identify: - Conflicting or redundant automations - Automations that create confusion or maintenance risk - Areas where automation should be removed or simplified - Known and potential failure points - Where automation lacks visibility or recovery options - The objective is to stabilize and simplify the system before expanding it. 2️⃣ System Role Definition Define and document: - GoHighLevel as the primary customer hub - What data and actions belong in GHL vs Jobber vs Zapier - Where automation should exist and where it should not - Where human decisions are required - How staff can safely override or bypass automation without breaking workflows This must clearly answer: “When something happens, which system owns the next step — and why?” 3️⃣ Customer Journey Segmentation (Critical Requirement) The system must support three distinct workflows: A) New Prospects (New Customers) Customers entering the business for the first time. Goals: - Expectation setting - Education and pricing alignment - Qualification before scheduling - Self-selection of poor-fit leads B) Existing Customers Requesting Additional Services Customers already in our system requesting new work. Goals: - Faster response - Reduced qualification friction - Clear estimate and follow-up tracking - Visibility into outstanding opportunities C) Design / Build Projects (Separate Workflow) Design/build projects follow a different timeline and decision process. Goals: - Complete intake and clarity - Design agreement milestones - Visibility across sales, design, and CS teams - Design workflows should prioritize flexibility and visibility over automation density. 4️⃣ Intake Form & Routing Design Because all prospects currently enter through GoHighLevel, Phase 0 must include: - Design of a clear intake form for new prospects - Definition of intake questions that determine customer journey routing - Identification of required vs optional intake information - Clear routing logic that places leads into the correct pipeline automatically - The intake process should improve qualification without creating friction for prospects. Phase 0 Deliverables - Written system architecture recommendations - Defined customer journeys and routing logic - Recommended pipeline structure - Intake form structure and routing rules - Automation boundaries (what to build vs not build) Owner approval before Phase 1 begins 🔹 Phase 1 — Implementation & Documentation Implementation must follow approved Phase 0 architecture. A) Pipeline & Automation Implementation Implement workflows including: - Lead entry and routing into correct customer journey - Automated inquiry confirmation with expectation-setting and FAQs - Qualification messaging including general pricing guidance - Pipeline automations and internal notifications - Post-install automation sending appropriate care guides (plants, sod, seed installation) when tagged by staff - Check-in automation for months 1, 6, and 11 after install to demonstrate care. Customers get a 1-year warranty for work that we perform - Stabilization or removal of problematic existing automations - Automation must be simple to trigger and safe to override by non-technical staff. B) SOP Documentation & System Transparency (Critical Requirement) Documentation is a primary deliverable of this project. The consultant must document: - System Understanding - How the system is structured - Why automation exists where it does - How GHL, Jobber, and Zapier are connected Operational SOPs: - How leads move through each pipeline - When staff work in GHL vs Jobber - How automations are triggered - How staff override or bypass automation safely Automation Documentation For each automation created: - What triggers it - What actions it performs - What systems are involved - Where failures may occur - How to test it Failure Testing & Recovery Paths (Required) The consultant must: - Identify common failure scenarios - Simulate failure conditions where possible - Document recovery paths step-by-step - Provide clear instructions for non-technical staff to diagnose and resolve issues - The goal is for another person to be able to understand, troubleshoot, and maintain the system without the original consultant. IMPLEMENTATION CONSTRAINTS: - GoHighLevel serves as the primary customer hub - Separate pipelines for different customer journeys - Simplicity over automation density - Native integrations preferred - No duplicate sources of truth - Automation should educate and qualify early, not pressure decisions - System must remain operable after consultant exit ACCEPTANCE CRITERIA Project is complete when: - Separate pipelines for the three customer journeys are operational - Lead qualification and expectation-setting workflows function correctly - Existing automation conflicts are resolved - Intake form and routing logic are operational - Automations have documented failure scenarios and recovery paths - SOPs exist for operation, override, testing, and failure recovery - A non-technical team member can operate workflows using documentation alone BUDGET Total Project Budget: Up to $3,500 (Fixed Price) Phase 0: $1,500 Phase 1: $2,000 Scope is intentionally focused on lead qualification, customer journey design, implementation, and documentation. Additional automation may be scoped separately. REQUIRED IN PROPOSAL Please answer: 1. How do you document automation systems so another person can maintain them? 2. How do you design automation that can safely fail without breaking workflows? 3. Describe a time you simplified an automation system instead of expanding it. FINAL NOTE This project is about removing complexity, not adding it. We are looking for someone who designs systems for humans, not tools. more about "Go High Level Workflow Architect Specialist"

Description: Overview I’m building a consumer SaaS platform called LoTox Lifestyle. This is not a content site, ecommerce store, or marketing app. LoTox is a decision system that helps people make cleaner, more evidence-informed lifestyle choices without overwhelm. The core product combines: A public, science-grounded content hub A paid, personalized system that generates user-specific plans from structured intake data AI used only for drafting explanations — never for making decisions This project lives or dies by logic, trust, and correctness. What You’ll Be Working On You will be implementing (not inventing) a system that includes: Authentication and account flows Multi-step intake questionnaire (rules-driven, not exploratory) A deterministic personalization engine (rule-based logic) AI-assisted plan generation with strict guardrails A user dashboard that presents personalized plans clearly Subscription gating (Stripe or equivalent) Admin interfaces for managing products, evidence, and rules Important: I will provide the decision logic, standards, and constraints. Your role is to implement them cleanly, not reinterpret them. What This Is (and Is Not) This is: A logic-heavy, judgment-sensitive system An iterative SaaS product A collaboration between product thinking and engineering A project where correctness matters more than speed This is not: A marketing website A blog or content CMS A “let the AI figure it out” application A fixed-scope, one-off build Core Technical Expectations You should be very comfortable with: Rule-based or workflow-driven systems Building guardrails around AI (no hallucinated outputs) React / Next.js (or equivalent modern frontend) Supabase, Firebase, or similar backend services Stripe subscriptions APIs, webhooks, and automation-friendly architectures Bonus (not required): Make.com or Zapier-style automation Prior AI SaaS or personalization platforms How We’ll Work Hourly engagement Phase-based milestones Clear written logic and acceptance criteria Paid trial task before long-term engagement Fast feedback, low ego, high ownership You’ll be expected to ask smart questions, flag edge cases, and avoid overengineering. I’m not coming into this cold. I’ve already designed the core system logic, decision rules, and UX flow, and have an early MVP scaffold in place that we’ll use as a foundation. I’m looking for a senior engineer who enjoys collaborating with a product-driven founder, respects existing logic, and can help implement and harden the system cleanly rather than reinvent it. To Apply (Required) Please answer all three questions below: Describe a project where logic and workflows mattered more than UI. How do you prevent AI hallucinations in production systems? If you had 30–45 days to ship an MVP, what would you intentionally not build? Applications that don’t thoughtfully answer these will not be considered. Final Note This product is being built carefully and intentionally. If you enjoy building systems where decisions must be explainable, auditable, and trustworthy, you’ll enjoy this work. more about "Senior Product Engineer (Freelance) — AI + Rules-Based Personalization SaaS"

Description: ABOUT US We're a small nonprofit startup on a mission to fundamentally renew American democracy—not from the left or the right, but from the ground up. Our vision is bold, it resonates across the political spectrum, and we’re going to introduce it to the world by releasing a groundbreaking documentary this July 4th. You can learn more about what we're building at https://joinofbyfor.org/. You can also see a rough cut of our upcoming documentary here: https://private.hellodemocracy.film/?r=UpWork&f=n Password: democracy THE ROLE We're looking for a sharp, reliable, and self-directed executive assistant to work directly with our Executive Director. You'll be a true right hand—someone who can take ownership of whatever needs doing, exercise good judgment, and free up the ED to focus on the work only they can do. To start, your primary focus will be building and refining an AI-assisted email system and managing the ED's calendar. The email system will use AI-powered tools to draft personalized responses at scale, with the goal of continuously improving workflows so less and less human editing is needed over time. This is a startup, and the right person will quickly become someone we rely on across operations, project coordination, compliance, and whatever else comes up. WHAT YOU'LL DO RIGHT AWAY - Build and refine our AI-assisted email system—triage inboxes, draft replies, improve workflows - Send routine emails independently; queue higher-stakes drafts for ED review - Manage the ED's calendar and handle scheduling - Participate in brief daily syncs (15 min) with everything organized for quick review and decisions - Proactively flag questions, surface decisions that need ED input, and keep the ED accountable ONGOING & GROWING - Continuously improve email workflows so the system gets better over time - Create clear Loom videos for async coordination - Help with administrative tasks, light bookkeeping, compliance, HR, and special projects as they come up - Take on new responsibilities as the organization scales toward our July launch and beyond WHAT WE'RE LOOKING FOR TECH-SAVVY AND SYSTEMS-MINDED. You're experienced and comfortable with tools that require logic-based thinking—AI prompts, database filters, workflow automation, project management tools, etc. You don't need experience with our specific tools, but you should be able to learn new ones quickly. STRONG JUDGMENT AND DISCRETION. You'll be representing our organization in writing and making calls about what needs ED review vs. what you can handle independently. You need to get this right. EXCELLENT COMMUNICATION AND EDITING SKILLS. Every communication we send reflects on the organization. You can write clearly, edit AI drafts effectively, and communicate warmly and professionally with people from all walks of life and across the political spectrum. And you can communicate with the ED clearly in meetings and in Loom videos. SELF-DIRECTED BUT NOT SILOED. You find answers yourself before asking questions—but you also know when something genuinely needs clarification and you speak up. You don't waste the ED's time with things you can figure out yourself, but you also don't go rogue. RELIABLE AND ORGANIZED. You show up consistently, keep things from falling through the cracks, pay close attention to details, and document your work so others can follow it. MISSION-ALIGNED. This isn't a role where the mission is background noise. The people you'll be corresponding with care deeply about democracy, and you need to as well. You should be personally invested in what we're building, not just willing to do the work. PRACTICAL DETAILS COMPENSATION: $40/hour. We're a grassroots nonprofit where everyone—including leadership—works at mission-aligned rates because we believe in what we're building. For context, the founder/ED's compensation works out to slightly less. EXPERIENCE: We're looking to hire an Expert-level Executive Assistant and prioritizing Top Rated and Top Rated Plus freelancers with strong Job Success scores and reviews. If you're new to Upwork but have substantial experience elsewhere, address that in your Loom. And if you're not an Expert-level EA with clear strengths in the areas we've outlined, then this isn't the right fit. HOURS: Expect 10–20 hours for an initial onboarding period (learning systems, clearing backlog, getting set up). After onboarding, we'll have a brief daily sync (15 min) at a consistent standing time. You'll complete your solo work flexibly before each sync so everything is ready for review. Eventually most of our day-to-day coordination will shift from syncs to Loom videos. Initially (after onboarding) plan to reserve roughly 1 hour per day; we will guarantee a minimum of 5 hours/week. As the role grows and we scale toward our July launch, we expect both the scope and hours to increase. SCHEDULE: Flexible, but you must be US-based with significant overlap to Eastern Time. START DATE: As soon as possible—though the hiring process will likely take a week or two. TIME TRACKING: You must be willing to use Upwork's time tracking/screenshot app (we don't typically allow manual tracking for digital work). GROWTH POTENTIAL: This is intended as a long-term role for the right person—not a task-based contract. As you prove capable and we grow, so does your role and your hours. We're a tiny team building something big, and we need people who can grow with us. TO APPLY Submit a Loom video introducing yourself, explaining why you're interested and why you'd be a good fit. Also tell us about your experience with AI tools and how you use them in your work. Just that—no cover letter. Applicants we think might be a good fit may be invited to complete a short practical exercise or two and have a call as part of the hiring process. more about "EA to Founder — Help Introduce a New Vision for Democracy in America"

Integrations required: * QuickBooks Online (Financial) - Sync clients, invoices, project references, payment status. * Asana (Project Management) - Auto-create project boards, sync tasks/status, push phase changes. * Google Drive (File Management) - Auto-provision folders for new clients/projects; store links back in Airtable. * G-Suite (Docs, Sheets, etc) - Template generation, etc. * DocuSign (Contract Signing) - Auto-create envelopes from Airtable records; sync signature status back. * PipeDrive (CRM) - Sync leads, opportunities, deal stages, and convert won deals into projects/clients in Airtable. The goal is zero duplicate data entry and fully automated lifecycle flow from lead → contract → project → invoicing. 3. Advisor Recruiting Tracking System We run a consulting model that relies on a network of expert Advisors. We need a full recruiting and tracking system, including: Advisor Pipeline System * Capture all potential Advisors via our research team’s recruiting efforts * Track outreach, conversations, interviews, and evaluations. • Maintain an up-to-date database of competencies, background, industry expertise. Assignment Engine * Match Advisors to specific client projects based on expertise, skills, availability, etc * Store all advisor engagement files and contracts in linked Google Drive folders. This system is a core component of our business, not a minor feature. It must be robust, well-designed, and smooth to operate. Requirements * Deep experience building complex Airtable systems with relational architecture. * Strong business operations understanding—consulting experience is a major plus. * Proven ability to integrate Airtable with external SaaS tools using: - Native integrations - Zapier / Make / Workato - Airtable Automations - Custom API scripts * Ability to design workflows that eliminate operational friction and enforce data consistency. * Clean, scalable schema design and documentation practices. * Experience with CRM/ERP/PSA systems is highly desirable. more about "Airtable Expert Needed to Build ERP + Automations + Advisor Recruiting System for Consulting Firm"

Description: We’re looking for a Monday CRM Product Expert to help us truly unlock the platform and design workflows that actually work for real teams — not just look good on paper. Our goal is to sit down with our different departments, understand how they’re currently using Monday, listen to their pain points, and translate those needs into clean, scalable, best-practice workflows. We need someone who can think creatively both inside the Monday platform and outside of it (using tools like Zapier or even custom development when needed) to streamline operational inefficiencies. What we want to build with Monday CRM: A full customer journey from prospect → deal → customer → repeat customer CRM workflows for sales tracking, quoting, invoicing, and purchase agreements (with signature support) Better sales communication workflows so our sales team can properly manage leads and prospects Dashboards that clearly show bottlenecks across departments, sales performance, and operational health Clean, logical board structures that actually reflect how our business operates Help with data cleanup, organization, and migration where needed We already have an in-house developer who can help with custom integrations or external systems. What we need from you is the Monday expertise — the ability to take what’s in our heads (and what our teams say) and translate it into a scalable Monday CRM setup that grows with the business. We know what we want operationally — we just don’t always know how to communicate that in “Monday language.” You’re a great fit if you are: Deeply experienced with Monday CRM (not just boards, but real CRM usage) Comfortable leading conversations with stakeholders and asking the right questions Able to design workflows that balance simplicity with scalability Familiar with integrations (Zapier, APIs, external tools, etc.) Friendly, communicative, and easy to work with Someone who enjoys collaborating directly with teams and translating chaos into clarity & who can document every SOP as they go. This is a hands-on, collaborative role. You’ll be working directly with our team members — sometimes one-on-one, sometimes as a group — to design workflows that actually make their lives easier. If you love turning messy operations into clean systems and making Monday really work for a growing business, we’d love to talk. more about "Monday CRM Product Expert"

Generic “AI strategy” decks or tool demos, like for venture investing or deck creation 4. Prompt libraries or simple training (e.g. AI 101) ---------------------------------------- THIS IS A FIT FOR YOU IF: 1. Built a CEO-ready AI system that gets used weekly (not once) 2. Created distinct advisor personas that actually challenge thinking (not same-answer personas) 3. Can teach a structured prompting approach (e.g., CRIT: Context, Role, Interview, Task) in plain English, with examples leaders can reuse 4. Know how to keep AI grounded using a small set of curated “inputs” without turning it into a big systems project ---------------------------------------- Phase 1 (3–4 weeks | fixed price $1,500–$3,000) Phase 1 is about shipping a small set of ready-to-use assets that I can run as CEO during a real week. No long documents. No “program.” The output should be copy/paste tools and simple routines. DELIVERABLES CURRENTLY CONTEMPLATED: 1. My AI “Board of Advisors” (10–12 reusable personas) You will build 10–12 advisor personas that represent different ways of thinking (finance skeptic, execution realist, risk-checker, talent evaluator, contrarian, simplifier, etc.). Each persona must include: what it optimizes for, what it challenges, the questions it always asks, and when to use it. 2. A “default prompting system” I can use every day (8–10 master prompts). Not a library. A short set of master prompts built around a structured method (like CRIT) that reliably produces strong outputs. Each master prompt must include: what it’s for, what inputs it needs from me, and how to run the “Interview” step so the AI asks clarifying questions before answering. 3. A CEO problem-solving toolkit. Create 6–8 simple templates that I can apply to hard problems repeatedly. Examples: options/tradeoffs, pre-mortem, bottleneck diagnosis, second-order effects, “what would change my mind,” and a lightweight EV/ROI frame. Each template must come with the matching master prompt so I can run it quickly. 4. Curated “ground truth inputs” + a lightweight routine Define the small set of internal inputs that should ground the AI (what matters, what doesn’t, and what not to include). Provide a simple weekly routine (15 minutes) for keeping those inputs current. The goal is practical grounding, not a data project. 4. A rollout plan for me (CEO) + a 60-day exec followup. A simple plan that shows exactly how I’ll use the assets, what I’ll capture, and what we’ll change based on results. Then a plan to onboard 2–3 execs after we validate. ---------------------------------------- HOW WE WILL SELECT: We’re optimizing for signal and we want to be fair with your time. Work samples required. No samples = no interview. Shortlisted candidates may get paid for a micro-audition ($250–$500). ---------------------------------------- HOW TO APPLY: Please submit only the following - PLEASE NO LONG INTRODUCTION: 1, A short note (150–200 words): who you’ve helped, what you built, and what leaders did differently week-to-week. 2. One mini-plan (bullets) for Phase 1 over 4 weeks more about "Executive AI Strategy Advisor to create a CEO “AI Thought Partner” — not engineering, not marketing"

Description: Hi. I’m Iooking for someone who can create a system by which a call to a phone number goes directly to a computer program which plays or generates a digital DTMF tone corresponding to a cell phone’s number zero (‘0’). (The zero tone consists of a 941 Hz low-frequency tone with a 1336 Hz high-frequency tone). For context, the scenario is that guest checking in to my Airbnb dials my number by pressing the call button associated with my name on the call box outside the apartment building. Normally, the call would ring on my cell phone, at which point I have to answer the call and then press zero on my phone’s keypad. The audio tone of the zero which I press causes the call box to automatically open the entry door of the apartment so that the guest can enter. My objective is to use in the call box a dedicated phone number which will go directly to a computer program which will play or generate the number zero for me. This way, I don’t have to physically answer the phone and press zero myself, but instead the process is automated. Although the call box is simply responding to the DTMF tone corresponding to the number zero, it seems that the sound has to be a digitally generated tone rather than a recording of the tone on my outgoing voicemail message. I’ve already tried recording the zero tone on my outgoing voicemail message, and the call box does not respond to it. However, online discussions pertaining to this issue state that a computer program using a digitally generated DTMF tone will work. more about "Simple Computer Program as an Automatic Response on Dedicated Phone Number"

Description: Project: Automated "Hiring Intent" Engine (Clay + HubSpot) The Objective We need a high-velocity outbound engine that detects specific hiring signals within our Enterprise Total Addressable Market and activates the buying committee immediately. The output we need to achieve: "When an enterprise account posts a relevant job, we identify it, map 100% of the buying committee, and have them queued for outreach within 48 hours of the posting." The Problem We Are Solving We are currently too slow or reactive. By the time we find out a major prospect is hiring for a critical role, they are already weeks into the process. We need to engage our target ICPs exactly at the moment the internal "pain" becomes public, when they open a requisition for a role we can solve. What We Need You to Build We need a fully automated Clay-to-HubSpot workflow that executes the following loop continuously: 1. The Signal Radar (Real-Time Detection) Monitor our specific list of Target Enterprise Accounts (we will provide the domain list). Detect active job postings for niche clinical/operational titles (e.g., Occupational Health Nurse, Site Medical Lead, Medical Assistant, Workplace Health Coordinator). Constraint: This cannot just be a "keyword match." It must filter out noise (e.g., standard hospital clinical roles) and identify roles specifically related to employee health & onsite clinics. 2. AI Qualification Use Clay’s AI Agent to read the job descriptions. Pass/Fail Logic: Does this job description mention specific compliance triggers (OSHA, Medical Surveillance) or onsite clinic management? If YES: Flag as "High Intent" and proceed. If NO: Discard immediately. 3. Activation and Committee Mapping Once a "High Intent" role is found, automatically map the Buying Committee for that specific account. Find verified contact info (Work Email + Mobile) for: VP/Director of Benefits CHRO Chief People Officer Director of Health & Safety Corporate Medical Director COO Goal: We need 100% coverage of these key decision-makers, not just one contact. 4. The Handoff and HubSpot Sync Create/Update the Company and Contacts in HubSpot. Crucial Step: Push a "Signal Context" note into HubSpot for our sales team. Also, Create Automated email outreach for speed-to-lead and personalized outreach Example: "Hiring a Site Medical Lead (Posted 2 days ago). Key responsibility listed: 'Manage onsite OSHA reporting'." Trigger an alert/task so our team can launch the sequence immediately. Success Metrics for This Build Latency: The time from "Job Posted" to "Lead in HubSpot" must be under 48 hours. Accuracy: AI Qualification must filter out at least 90% of irrelevant/noise job postings. Completeness: The Buying Committee map must return valid contacts for at least 3 key stakeholders per account. Tech Stack Core: Clay CRM: HubSpot Enrichment: Your recommended waterfall Please reply with: A brief explanation of how you structure your "Job Scrape to AI Qualify" logic in Clay. Estimated timeline to get a functional Pilot live (we are moving fast). Project cost estimate. more about "Project: Automated "Hiring Intent" Engine (Clay + HubSpot)"

This is not a paper-pushing PM role. You will be in the lab, on calls with spoke contractors, reviewing technical deliverables, and making daily execution decisions. You must be comfortable with ambiguity, able to drive progress without constant direction, and experienced enough to flag risks before they become problems. You are expected to have deep domain expertise in at least one of the core technical areas (hardware, biology, fluidics, or software) and strong enough logic and common sense to coordinate across all of them. We operate AI-first. AI proficiency is not optional; it is a mandatory requirement. You must be able to use AI tools (Claude, ChatGPT, or equivalent) to rapidly prototype deliverables, validate assumptions with data, and accelerate every stage of project execution. We expect a detail-oriented, data-driven operator who treats QA/QC as a core discipline: you identify problems early, surface them proactively, and propose solutions before being asked. Key Responsibilities Project Execution & Tracking • Own the execution of the project implementation plan end-to-end • Break down high-level milestones into weekly deliverables with clear owners and dependencies • Maintain a live project tracker: status, blockers, burn rate, critical path • Run weekly status cycles: collect updates from all spokes, compile, brief PI in under 5 minutes • Conduct milestone reviews with written acceptance criteria Contractor & Vendor Management • Manage 3–5 spoke contractors (circuit design, fluidics, CRO, software) • Evaluate incoming contractor proposals using structured scoring rubrics • Run technical screening calls and produce contractor comparison matrices • Monitor contractor deliverables against SOW milestones; escalate delays immediately • Coordinate integration across hardware, biology, and software workstreams Technical Oversight • Review technical deliverables for quality, completeness, and alignment with specs • Manage project budget: track burn rate, forecast spend, flag overruns before they happen • Maintain SOPs, version control, and documentation standards across the team • Support integration testing when hardware, fluidics, and biology components converge Required Qualifications • 5+ years of hands-on experience in hardware product development (not just oversight - you have personally built, tested, or shipped physical products) • Deep domain expertise in at least one core area: electrical/hardware engineering, mechanical/fluidics systems, molecular biology, or software/AI systems • Direct experience in medical devices, biotech instrumentation, consumer electronics, or defense hardware • Proven track record managing external contractors and vendor relationships with measurable outcomes • Experience with milestone-based project tracking and risk management (Gantt, critical path, earned value, or equivalent) • Mandatory AI proficiency: you use AI tools (Claude, ChatGPT, or equivalent) daily to draft, analyze, prototype, and validate. This is not a nice-to-have; it is required to keep pace with our workflow • Exceptionally detail-oriented and data-driven: you catch errors others miss, you back decisions with data, and you document everything • Strong QA/QC mindset: you proactively identify problems, flag risks early, and propose solutions without waiting to be asked • Strong written communication: you can produce a status report a PI reads in 5 minutes • Comfortable operating in a fast-paced, ambiguous environment with minimal supervision • Must be based in New Hampshire or willing to relocate to Hanover, NH area before start date • Available to start within 2 weeks of contract execution Required: Hands-On Experience Portfolio In addition to your CV, you must submit a Hands-On Experience Document (2–4 pages) covering: 1. Product Case Study: Describe one hardware product you personally took from concept to prototype or production. Include your specific role, team size, timeline, budget, and outcome. 2. Contractor Management Example: Describe a project where you managed 2+ external contractors simultaneously. What tracking system did you use? How did you handle a missed deliverable or quality issue? 3. Technical Decision: Describe a situation where you had to make a technical trade-off under time pressure. What was the trade-off, what data did you use, and what was the result? 4. Failure & Recovery: Describe a project that went wrong and how you recovered. What would you do differently? This document replaces the traditional cover letter. We evaluate candidates on what they have built, not what they claim they can do. Preferred Qualifications • Experience with neural interfaces, MEA systems, brain-computer interfaces, or organoid technology • Background in microfluidics, bioreactor systems, or lab-on-chip platforms • Familiarity with FDA regulatory pathways (510(k), De Novo, IDE) • Experience with DARPA, ARPA-H, NIH BRAIN, or similar government research programs • Master’s or PhD in biomedical engineering, electrical engineering, mechanical engineering, or related field • Prior experience in an academic research lab or university-affiliated research center Contract Terms • 3-month initial contract with 2-week termination notice • 2-week structured trial period with measurable deliverables • Month 3 performance review: convert to permanent, extend contract, or end engagement • All intellectual property assigned to Dartmouth College • Onsite or remote in Hanover, NH. Code quality & version control - Clean code practices: write modular, testable code following SOLID principles and participate in code reviews to ensure consistency - Testing & QA: write unit and integration tests (TDD) and assist in running performance and load tests to maintain platform stability - Git workflow: manage daily tasks through Git, including branching, creating detailed pull requests, and resolving merge conflicts in a shared repository 4. Growth & operational Support - Resource monitoring: support monitoring of API token usage and cloud resource consumption to maintain cost-efficiency - Technical troubleshooting: independently research technical hurdles using documentation and AI tools before escalating - Collaborative syncs: Participate in technical syncs, providing updates on progress and contributing ideas more about "Software Engineer"

URL: https://www.upwork.com/jobs/Python-Developer-for-Race-Series-Timing-System_~022020903815876434025/?referrer_url_path=find_work_home
Description: **Project Overview** This project involves developing a simple, reliable desktop Python application for a non-profit running group's race series (multiple events). The system supports: - Upfront one-time payment for the entire race series via Stripe (required before any participation). - Registration only after successful payment. - Per-race barcode generation and printing (unique to each race entry for paid/registered runners). - Mass start: All runners begin at the same fixed gun time. - Finish-line timing: USB barcode scanner reads the label to record individual finish time and calculate elapsed time from start. - No on-site payments—only registration, printing, scanning, and logging. The application is an MVP/prototype for real race-day use on a Windows laptop (cross-platform compatibility preferred). It must be easy to run with minimal setup, well-documented, and suitable for non-technical race organizers. **Key Functional Requirements** 1. **Registration & Upfront Payment Flow** - Collect runner details (name, email, etc.). - Create a Stripe payment session for the full series fee. - On successful payment confirmation: Mark the runner as paid/registered in the database. - Prevent barcode printing for unpaid runners. 2. **Race-Day Preparation** - Select or create a race event (set fixed start time). - For paid/registered runners: Generate a race-specific barcode (e.g., Code 128 encoding runner ID + race identifier). - Print the barcode on a thermal label/wristband using ESC/POS commands. 3. **Finish-Line Timing** - Real-time listening for USB barcode scanner input (keyboard-emulation mode). - On scan: Lookup runner and race entry. - Record finish timestamp. - Calculate and store elapsed time (finish - fixed start). - Handle basics like duplicate scans or errors. 4. **Database & Reporting** - Use SQLite (local file-based): - Runners table (unique key: name/email, payment status, series details). - Race entries table (race ID/date, barcode value, start/finish times, elapsed). - Simple export: CSV of results for posting/sharing. **Technical Stack & Guidelines** - Python 3.x (standard libraries prioritized). - Barcode generation: python-barcode (Code 128 format recommended). - Printing: escpos-python or equivalent for thermal printers (e.g., Phomemo M110 USB/Bluetooth). - Scanner input: Capture via input() or pynput (keyboard emulation). - Payments: Official stripe Python library (Checkout sessions or PaymentIntents for one-time upfront charge). - Database: Built-in sqlite3 module. - Error handling: Graceful messages for offline hardware, payment failures, etc. - Code style: Clean, modular, commented; include README with pip requirements.txt, setup, and hardware connection steps. - Optional: Basic CLI menu or Tkinter GUI for ease of use. **Deliverables** 1. Complete Python script(s) or small application covering full flow. 2. Sample SQLite database with test data. 3. Installation/usage instructions (README.md). 4. 1–2 short demo videos or screen recordings (payment → registration → print → scan → logged time). 5. Tested compatibility notes for hardware (NetumScan NSL3 USB scanner, Phomemo M110 printer). more about "Python Developer for Race Series Timing System"

Description: Personal Assistant or Project Manager – Operations, Freelancers & AI (Remote) A proactive, detail‑driven personal assistant is sought to hire, coordinate and oversee other freelancers, and intelligently leverage AI tools across multiple projects. This role blends classic executive support with light operations management and AI‑enabled problem solving, with the goal of keeping priorities clear, projects moving, and communications tight. ​ Role overview The assistant will act as a central coordination hub: consolidating information, and ensuring that freelance contributors deliver on brief, on time, and to a high standard. The ideal candidate is comfortable switching between strategic thinking (what should happen next?) and hands‑on execution (drafting, following up, documenting, and improving systems). ​ Key responsibilities Coordinate and manage a small team of freelancers (writers, designers, researchers, editors, etc.), including posting roles, screening candidates, scheduling interviews, tracking deliverables, and following up on deadlines. Maintain simple but robust systems (docs, sheets, task boards, checklists) so projects, links, decisions, and assets are easy to find and nothing important is dropped. ​ Use AI tools (e.g., ChatGPT, Claude, Notion AI, Google Workspace AI) to draft emails, summarize documents, create briefs, outline content, and propose options or solutions. Proactively identify bottlenecks, risks, and decision points, flag them early, and suggest concrete next steps rather than waiting for instruction. ​ Draft, refine, and standardize SOPs for recurring processes (hiring, onboarding freelancers, content workflows, simple reporting), updating them as tools and needs evolve. ​ Communicate clearly and regularly via written updates, summarizing progress, issues, and tradeoffs so decisions are fast and well‑informed. ​ Required qualities The successful candidate will demonstrate: Problem solving and resourcefulness at a high level: you compare options, think several steps ahead, and find ways through ambiguity instead of getting stuck. ​ Strong initiative: you naturally take the “next intelligent action,” anticipate needs, and volunteer useful ideas or process improvements without being asked. ​ Systems thinking: you enjoy turning messy inputs into clear workflows, templates, and checklists, and you see how separate projects and freelancers connect to a bigger picture. Excellent written communication: you write concise, structured messages and summaries, and you can document reasoning so others can quickly understand context and decisions. ​ Calm, direct professionalism: you are comfortable giving and receiving feedback, escalating concerns, and holding freelancers accountable while staying respectful and solutions‑oriented. Skills and experience Prior experience as a virtual assistant, executive assistant, project coordinator, or operations‑oriented freelancer supporting founders, executives, or creators. Demonstrated ability to manage or coordinate other freelancers or team members in a remote environment. ​ Proficiency with core productivity tools (Google Workspace or Microsoft 365, task managers, cloud storage) and comfort picking up new software quickly. ​ Practical experience using AI tools in real workflows: drafting content, structuring information, doing first‑pass research, or automating repetitive tasks. ​ Strong organization, time management, and attention to detail; you keep multiple threads moving without dropping details or needing constant reminders. ​ Cultural fit and how to apply This role is best suited to someone who enjoys working closely with a high‑engagement founder, values autonomy, and takes pride in making complex work feel simple, structured, and under control. Curiosity, integrity, and long‑term thinking are essential, as is genuine interest in using AI and systems to create leverage rather than just “checking boxes.” ​ To apply, please submit: A brief background (no CV needed at this stage) highlighting your most relevant personal assistant / operations / coordination experience. 1–2 concrete examples where you managed other freelancers or team members, solved a non‑obvious problem, or significantly improved a process. ​ A short section (approximately 300–400 words) explaining: Why this specific assistant‑plus‑operations role is highly appealing to you. ​ What personal characteristics make you a strong cultural fit (values, working style, communication style, relationship to autonomy and ownership). ​ How you currently use AI tools in your work, and how you see them evolving the way assistants and small teams operate over the next few years. ​ Applications that do not address the cultural‑fit and AI‑usage questions in depth will not be considered, as alignment on mindset and working style is a critical part of this role. more about "Virtual Assistant with AI and Operations Project Management Skills"

URL: https://www.upwork.com/jobs/Client-Facing-Delivery-Partner-Enterprise-Systems_~022017674353865125957/?referrer_url_path=find_work_home
Description: We are seeking an independent, client-facing delivery partner to support complex enterprise technology initiatives within a growing advisory firm. This role suits someone who operates autonomously in fast-moving environments, translates ambiguity into action, and moves work forward without step-by-step direction. You will manage multiple concurrent workstreams and competing priorities across stakeholders and timelines, maintaining visibility, identifying dependencies, and making informed tradeoff decisions as conditions change. We are seeking someone who can join an active engagement and take ownership quickly. You should expect to get up to speed quickly, own your ramp-up after onboarding, and proactively handle the planning, scheduling, tracking, follow-ups, and communication required to keep work moving. Success in this role comes from acting on what’s in front of you, accounting for shifting decisions around you, and building enough context to make sound judgment calls as you go. This engagement requires 20–30 hours per week, with flexibility by project phase. Engagement Scope & Responsibilities Delivery & Workstream Management * Act as a primary delivery partner on client initiatives. * Translate discussions into clear decisions, actions, and next steps. * Manage multiple concurrent workstreams across client teams and third parties. * Maintain visibility into priorities, sequencing, and dependencies. * Identify risks, conflicts, and bottlenecks; propose adjustments to keep work moving. * Maintain delivery plans, action logs, and decision records. * Re-prioritize dynamically as conditions change. Documentation & Communication * Create documentation, templates, or SOPs only where needed to reduce friction. * Keep documentation current as work evolves. * Prepare concise, decision-oriented updates for leadership and clients. * Support meeting preparation and recommend cadence or structure changes when needed. What We’re Looking For * Experience delivering outcomes in complex, fast-moving environments. * Strong independent judgment and comfort progressing without full information. * Ability to prioritize execution amid change. * Proficiency in presentation creation and Excel. * Experience in advisory, professional services, or enterprise technology preferred. * Willingness to support occasional travel. How Success Is Measured Success in this engagement will be evaluated by: * Forward progress across agreed delivery objectives. * Clear capture of decisions, actions, and next steps. * Fewer unresolved dependencies and execution delays. * Effective translation of discussion into action. * Sustained momentum across multiple parallel workstreams, even as priorities shift. Proposals that answer the questions below will be prioritized. 1. When you join a project already in motion and documentation is incomplete, what do you take ownership of immediately, and what do you intentionally defer? 2. Describe a situation where priorities shifted quickly. What did you change first to keep work moving? 3. When multiple issues compete for attention, how do you decide what to act on now versus what can wait? 4. Think of a time delivery started to stall. What was the first thing you personally did to restore momentum? 5. What aspects of delivery work do you find most challenging, and how do you manage that in practice? 6. When responsible for multiple workstreams with competing priorities, how do you prevent items from drifting or getting stuck? 7. When you join a project that’s already in motion and nothing is fully documented, what do you tend to focus on first, and why? Give a high level 7 day plan approach. 8. Are you available for occasional travel? 9. We operate primarily in U.S. Eastern Time. What is your general weekday and weekend availability? more about "Client-Facing Delivery Partner (Enterprise Systems)"

Participants need only to talk over the phone for about 15 min (absolutely under 20 min) as we ask a few questions about the space, products, and the interviewee's general attitudes. I will use the native Upwork scheduling tool to set up a time. Phone calls only, no zooms for ease To restate : you will need to chat with us over the phone for about 15 min, max 20, and have a conversation about areas / ideas where you think AI and automation SHOULD be happening in your own work, but havent yet. Please only reply if you are interested and have experience with this space. URL: https://www.upwork.com/jobs/Output-Evaluation-Specialist-Needed_~022019470637539515459/?referrer_url_path=find_work_home
Description: We are seeking a detail-oriented professional to review and evaluate AI-generated outputs based on strict evaluation criteria. The role requires exceptional attention to detail and availability to conduct 15+ hours of evaluation per week on an ongoing basis. DESCRIPTION OF ACTIVITY This job involves evaluating a high volume of AI-generated natural language outputs. Outputs you will review include text passages, chat exchanges, statements of fact, and recommendations. Your job will be to identify specific types of issues in these outputs based on defined criteria, and then label and describe each issue. You will work inside a proprietary evaluation platform that displays the data for your review and includes fields for you to input evaluation. NOTE ON AI USE You may not use AI to assist you in completing this work in any way, as this violates both our security policy and the core methodology of this work. SERVICE LEVEL AGREEMENT To maintain the rapid feedback loop necessary for this work, we adhere to a 48-hour turnaround time (SLA) for all evaluations. While you have flexibility in choosing when you work your 15+ hours per week, this is not a role where hours can be batched into a single day. Reviewers are expected to log into the task queue at least 2-3 times per work week (ie. roughly every other day) to clear pending tasks. REQUIREMENTS Candidates must: - be located in the US - have strong ability to read and analyze content written in English - bring exceptional attention to detail - adhere to strict evaluation criteria involving analysis of natural language - pass background check and adhere to very strict security/confidentiality requirements All candidates must have at least one prior job in AI evaluation. Please make sure to describe related prior work in your cover letter. more about "AI Output Evaluation Specialist Needed"
